# PART 1 — Your unit section(s) of FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 (verbatim)

## Chapter 23 — Yizhuang Liu 的严格因子化研究 / The rigorous factorization research of Yizhuang Liu

`\label{ch:yzliu}` · `ch23_yzliu.tex` · **15 pp** · frontier format

**Name and biography rule.** "Yizhuang Liu" is never transliterated into Chinese characters (unverified; claim A02); no career or biographical information beyond an affiliation printed in a cited paper (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §2 item 3, §6.9).

**Purpose.** Present, with explicit hypotheses and honest grades, the part of TMD and LaMET factorization theory that Yizhuang Liu (alone and with collaborators) turned into theorem-level statements: (i) the Euclidean realization of TMD soft physics — the off-light-cone soft function as a heavy-quark form factor, the partial-exponentiation lemma, pinch poles and ultrasoft modes; (ii) the analytic structure behind the CSS-type arguments — analyticity in the complex hyperbolic angle, universality classes, the factorization (linearity) of rapidity divergences, the intrinsic soft function, the 2024 time-ordering theorem; (iii) Liu's Collins-style power-counting proof route of the quasi-TMD theorem (stated in Ch22), the hard kernel, LFWF amplitudes, the meson form factor, DY in quasi-TMDs, Sivers ratios; (iv) threshold factorization of the quasi-PDF kernel with the heavy–light Sudakov kernel and the space-like jet function, the anomalous-dimension algebra and the linear renormalon; (v) GPDs near $x=\pm\xi$; (vi) exactly solvable 2D laboratories; (vii) extensions of the exponential-decay theorem. Liu's own assessment, to be quoted (`Liu:2022nnk` §6, read): "all the proofs in the paper, except the ones for the analyticity, follow the typical rigorous standards in the TMD factorization literature"; promoting them to "fully mathematically consistent proofs" requires a multi-scale analysis of singular Minkowski integrals.

**Depends on.** Ch3 (`def:prelim-wilson`); Ch2 (`def:prelim-qcd`, `def:prelim-brst`); Ch5 (`def:sudakov-cusp`); Ch14 (`def:tmd-soft`, `def:tmd-collins`, `rem:tmd-schemes`, `prop:tmd-regions`, `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, `lem:tmd-glauber`, `prop:tmd-evolution`, `thm:tmd-factorization`, `thm:tmd-sivers-sign`); Ch19 (`def:excl-gpd`); Ch21 (`def:power-borel`, `prop:power-renormalon-examples`, `prop:power-thooft-model`); Ch22 (`def:lattice-quasi`, `prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`, `prop:lamet-oneloop`, `prop:lamet-twoloop`, `thm:lattice-lamet`, `prop:lamet-lrr`, `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`, `def:lamet-quasi-gpd`, `asm:lamet-gap`, `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `asm:lamet-staple`, `def:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `def:lamet-soft-offlc`, `def:lamet-reduced-soft`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:lamet-tmd-ratios`); App B (`prop:appB-webs`, `prop:appB-cusp-oneloop`). Forward pointer (text only): Ch24 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm`.

**Box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」.**
- 证明了：`lem:yzliu-matrix-tree`、`lem:yzliu-final-state`（数学）；`prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop`、`prop:yzliu-gpd-oneloop`（[R, R_PT]，限于所述阶）；`cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair`；内容等级 [C]）；在所述模型中：`thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`（作为 QCD 陈述为 [P]）。

### Canonical statements

#### §23.2 `sec:yzliu-soft`

* **引理 `lem:yzliu-partial-exp` — 部分指数化引理** [A].
  - *Statement.* For $F(C,x,x')={\rm Tr}\langle O(x)\,W(C;x'\to x)\,O'(x')\rangle$ with a Wilson line of arbitrary piecewise-smooth shape $C$ and local operators at its end points,
    $$F(C,x,x')=e^{\Phi(C)}\;{\rm Tr}\langle O(x)W(C;x'\to x)O'(x')\rangle_{\rm 2PI},$$
    where $e^{\Phi(C)}$ is the trace-normalized vacuum expectation value of the line, written as the exponential of its webs (maximally non-abelian colour factors; `prop:appB-webs`), and the remainder is the sum of operator–link two-particle-irreducible vertices $w_0$ with modified colour factors; for closed loops with operator insertions the same holds with the replica trick.
  - `\uses{prop:appB-webs, def:prelim-wilson}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.3 pp): generalized eikonal identity $\sum_{G\in\mathcal G_d}I(G)=\frac1{S_d}\prod_{i=0}^{n(d)}I(w_i)$ with $w_0$ held fixed (`Liu:2022nnk` eq. (B.5), read in full), followed by the Gatheral / Frenkel–Taylor resummation (`Gatheral1983`, `FrenkelTaylor1984`). Placement note: App B may add a one-line remark pointing here (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.3 row B).
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: the combinatorial core is standard; the extension to a fixed operator–link vertex $w_0$ is stated to go through "similarly to the standard one" and is not written in detail.
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft` — 捏点奇点、超软模式与 $L\to\infty$ 极限** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) For the quasi-TMD staple, $e^{\Phi(\mathcal W_z)}=\langle0|\mathcal W_z|0\rangle\sim e^{-LE(b_\perp)}$ with $E=2\delta m+V(b_\perp)$, and the rectangular loop gives $Z_E(2L)\sim e^{-2LE}$; hence $\lim_{L\to\infty}\langle P|\tilde O_z|P\rangle/\sqrt{Z_E}$ exists order by order; the pinch of the two eikonal propagators $1/[(k^z+i0)(k^z-i0)]$ is exhibited by $z$-ordering the two staples ("single-line notation") and cancels in the ratio. (b) Ultrasoft modes: individual diagrams develop power-like IR divergences at high orders ($\propto1/L$), the analogue of the Appelquist–Dine–Muzinich effect in the static potential (there at $O(\alpha_s^4)$); they cancel after resummation of the 2PI dipole self-interactions into a dressed single-line propagator; the relevant scale is the octet–singlet splitting $V_8-V$, for the boosted soft function $\alpha_sC_Ae^{-Y}/(2b_\perp)$; $Y$-dependent "ultra-ultrasoft" modes are argued to cancel by colour neutrality and Ward identities. (c) Time-like, double-time-ordered soft functions have no pinch poles and are IR finite (induction on "4PI" webs).
  - `\uses{lem:yzliu-partial-exp, def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:lamet-soft-offlc}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.3 pp): `Liu:2022nnk` §3.2–3.3, §4.2–4.3, Apps. B, D (read). History (attribution, claim A04): dividing out self-interactions of off-light-cone staples with rectangular loops goes back to `Collins:2008ht` and `Ji:2018hvs` (abstract); (a) supplies the all-order justification — this is the all-order argument for (ST2) of `asm:lamet-staple`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: (b) the all-order cancellation is argued, not proven; (c) the induction assumes the absence of power IR divergences at lower orders. Do **not** state an exact first loop order of the ultrasoft effect for the quasi-TMD (the source's wordings differ: "beyond three loops", "from the 3-loop level"); quote the static-potential statement only.
* **假设 `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` — 静态色单态 $Q\bar Q$ 对的谱假设** [C]. Register entry: Part VII annex §F1.2 (= `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §3).
  - (HP1) In the colour-singlet static $Q\bar Q$ sector at fixed $\vec b_\perp$ there is an isolated ground state with residual energy $E=\gamma^{-1}V(\vec b_\perp)$; the continuum is gapped (excited states are projected out by Riemann–Lebesgue, or by $t\to t(1-i0)$).
  - (HP2) The pair interpolating field creates the ground state with a non-zero, boost-invariant overlap $\Phi(\vec b_\perp)$.
  - (HP3) Wilson-line self-energies and the $Q\bar Q$ time evolution cancel against $\sqrt{ZZ}$ (an LSZ-type amputation; perturbatively `lem:yzliu-partial-exp`).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc}`.
* **定理 `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet` — 离光锥软函数是快速色源对的形状因子** [A].
  - *Statement.* Order by order in PT, the off-light-cone soft function of `def:lamet-soft-offlc` equals the amputated correlator of two pairs of static colour sources $\psi_v,\eta_v$ (HQET fields with $\mathcal L=\psi_v^\dagger iv\cdot D\psi_v+\eta_v^\dagger iv\cdot D\eta_v$, our sign of $D$) with the current $J(\vec b_\perp,v,v')=\eta^\dagger_{v'}(\vec b_\perp)\eta_v(\vec b_\perp)\psi^\dagger_{v'}(0)\psi_v(0)$ inserted at $t=0$; because $J$ acts at equal time, $S$ has a Euclidean representation $S=\lim W_E/\sqrt{Z_EZ_E}$ computed with Euclidean HQET, $i\tilde v\cdot D_E=D_\tau-i\beta D_z$; $S$ depends on $Y+Y'$ only. `% VF-YZL-2`
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, lem:yzliu-partial-exp, asm:lamet-aux-renorm}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.3 pp): the HQET propagator is a Wilson line, so the soft function is the amputated correlator of the four static sources; since $J$ acts at equal time the correlator can be rotated to Euclidean time (`Ji:2019sxk` eqs. (1)–(13), read in full; `Liu:2022nnk` §4.1–4.2, App. A; `JiEtAl2021` §V.C); the one-loop equality is explicit (`ex:yzliu-soft-oneloop`).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (perturbative identity; the non-perturbative form-factor statement is `cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` below, content grade [C]). Lattice caveats stated by the authors: fermion doubling of static sources, renormalization of the velocity, large-velocity noise. Contrast (attribution E07(ii)): Euclidean "bent" soft functions (`Ji:2014hxa`, `Ebert:2019okf`; `Ebert:2022fmh` attribute the bent function to `Ji:2018hvs` and `Ebert:2019okf`) reproduce the one-loop logarithms, but all their cusps are Euclidean right angles, and the cusp anomalous dimension at $\pi/2$ differs from $\gamma_{HH}$ from two loops (`KorchemskyRadyushkin1987`): $\Gamma^{(2)}(\frac\pi2)-\gamma^{(2)}_{HH}=C_FC_A(\frac{\zeta_3}2-\frac{\pi^2}{24})(\frac{\alpha_s}\pi)^2\approx0.190\,C_FC_A(\frac{\alpha_s}\pi)^2$ ($n_f$ parts equal; VF-YZL-17), so their $\ln\mu^2b_\perp^2$ structure fails from two loops — the argument is that of `Ji:2019sxk` (p. 5; same remark in `Ji:2019ewn`), which `Ebert:2022fmh` report without an independent proof (E07(ii) confirmed; the explicit value of the two-loop mismatch is our evaluation of the known angle-dependent two-loop cusp anomalous dimension). Priority wording (A03 confirmed, item A1): 「据我们所知（INSPIRE 检索，2026 年 10 月），`Ji:2019sxk` 首次把 Collins 离光锥软因子表示为时间无关的矩阵元——快速重夸克对的形状因子；此前的弯折欧氏软函数只再现单圈对数」. Misconception M16: the $Y$-linear growth is the CS kernel of a physical form factor — rapidity divergences are physical.
* **例 `ex:yzliu-soft-oneloop` — 单圈离光锥软函数与 $S_r$** (no tag). $S^{(1)}=a[2-2(Y+Y')]L_b+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$; with the exact one-loop rapidity dependence $\tilde S^{(1)}=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(\mathcal Y\coth\mathcal Y-\sigma)L_b$ ($\mathcal Y$ = total rapidity difference; `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, VF-TMD-8): time-like lines with self-energies ($\sigma=1$) give $\mathcal D^{(1)}=2aL_b$, $S_r^{(1)}=-2aL_b$, $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$; space-like lines without self-interactions ($\sigma=0$) give $\mathcal D^{(1)}=0$ — the trap of `def:lamet-soft-offlc`. `\uses{thm:yzliu-soft-hqet, lem:tmd-soft-oneloop, def:lamet-reduced-soft}`. 0.2 pp.
* **推论 `cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` — 软函数是静态色源对的形状因子（非微扰形式）** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* 「（$R_{\mathrm{cond}}$：在所列编号假设之下证明完整；结论的地位不高于其中最弱的假设。）」「前提：\cref{asm:yzliu-heavy-pair}（及 \cref{thm:yzliu-soft-hqet}）。」
  - *Statement.* $S(b_\perp,Y,Y')={}_{v'}\langle Q\bar Q,\vec b_\perp|J(\vec b_\perp,v,v')|Q\bar Q,\vec b_\perp\rangle_v$, with $|Q\bar Q,\vec b_\perp\rangle_v$ the lowest colour-singlet static pair state at fixed separation $\vec b_\perp$, velocity $v$ and zero residual momentum, and $J$ the current of `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet`: the off-light-cone soft function is the elastic form factor of a fast colour-singlet pair of static sources. `% VF-YZL-2`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：\cref{asm:intro-wightman}（MA0）。
  - `\uses{asm:yzliu-heavy-pair, thm:yzliu-soft-hqet}`.
  - *Proof.* Full given (HP1)–(HP3) (0.2 pp): insert complete sets of heavy-pair states before and after $J$, take $t\to\infty$ (Riemann–Lebesgue, or $t\to t(1-i0)$) so that only the ground state survives (HP1), use its overlap (HP2) and divide by $\sqrt{ZZ}$ (HP3) (`Ji:2019sxk` eqs. (8), (11), read; `Liu:2022nnk` §4.1–4.2, App. A; `JiEtAl2021` §V.C).
  - *Status.* Content grade [C] (from `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair`); the extraction of $S_I$ on the lattice through the meson form factor (`prop:yzliu-meson-ff`) is the practical counterpart.
* **Figures.** `fig:yzliu-soft-hqet` (N9); optional `fig:yzliu-single-line`.

#### §23.3 `sec:yzliu-analyticity`

* **定义 `def:yzliu-hyperbolic-angle` — 双曲角与六种构型** (no tag).
  - Single-time-ordered, $Z$-subtracted soft functions $S(b_\perp,\mu;v_1,v_2)$ with arbitrary off-light-cone directions (time-like or space-like, past- or future-pointing; generalization of `def:lamet-soft-offlc`, `Liu:2022nnk` eq. (93)); hyperbolic angle $\theta(v_1,v_2)={\rm Arcosh}\frac{v_1\cdot v_2-i0}{\sqrt{v_1^2+i0}\sqrt{v_2^2+i0}}$; `tab:yzliu-theta` (VF-YZL-4): space-like/space-like past/past $\theta=Y$; time-like/time-like past/past $Y-i\pi$; time-like/time-like past/future $Y$; space-like/space-like past/future $Y+i\pi$; time-like/space-like past/future $Y+i\pi/2$; time-like/space-like past/past $Y-i\pi/2$. Maps: $\cosh Y=\frac{1+z^2}{1-z^2}$ (DY) or $\frac{z^2+1}{z^2-1}$ (form factor). `% VF-YZL-4`
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **假设 `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` — 维数正规化微扰论中的 Wightman 结构** [A]. Register entry: Part VII annex §F1.2 (= `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §3).
  - (WP1) Order by order in PT, in linear covariant gauges and with DR at generic $\varepsilon\ne0$, the gluon and ghost Wightman functions satisfy the spectral condition and local commutativity (covariant operator formalism, `KugoOjima1979`); hence their coordinate-space representations are boundary values of functions holomorphic in the (permuted) extended tubes, covariant under proper complex Lorentz transformations (Bargmann–Hall–Wightman, `Streater:1989vi`).
  - (WP2) Null separations on the same light-like line give scaleless contributions in DR, so the regulator $\eta\to0^+$ may be taken at the integrand level.
  - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst}`.
* **定理 `thm:yzliu-analyticity` — 软函数对复双曲角的解析性** [A].
  - *Statement.* Order by order in PT (DR, linear covariant gauge), the single-time-ordered subtracted soft function depends on the directions only through $\theta(v_1,v_2)$ and is the boundary value of one function $S(\theta)$ holomorphic in $\vert{\rm Im}\,\theta\vert<\pi$ and continuous up to $|{\rm Im}\,\theta|=\pi$; the six configurations of `tab:yzliu-theta` are its boundary values. Consequences: (i) $S_{\rm DY}(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=S_{\rm FF}(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')$ — Collins' space-like (tilted, double-time-ordered) DY soft factor equals the form-factor soft function of `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet`; (ii) for half-on/half-off light-cone soft functions $S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta,\hat z)=S(b_\perp,\mu,-i\delta,\hat t)$; (iii) in the $\delta$ regulator $S^+(b_\perp,\mu,\delta,\delta)=S^{-\dagger}\big(b_\perp,\mu,\frac{i\delta}{1+i0},\frac{i\delta}{1+i0}\big)$, i.e. $\ln\frac{\mu^2}{2\delta^+\delta^-}\tilde K\to(\ln\frac{\mu^2}{2\delta^+\delta^-}+i\pi)\tilde K$; (iv) the DY and SIDIS soft factors are equal in the standard $\delta$ regulator (Hartogs' theorem on separately holomorphic functions). `% VF-YZL-4`
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-hyperbolic-angle, asm:yzliu-wightman-pt, thm:yzliu-soft-hqet, def:tmd-soft}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.5 pp): `Liu:2022nnk` §4.5–4.7, Apps. F, H (read). Route 1 (spectral): energy positivity ⇒ analyticity in $v^0\to z$ in a half-plane; at $z=-iv_0$ both functions become Euclidean Wilson loops equal by $(\tau,z)$ rotation invariance; uniqueness of continuation ⇒ $g(z)=f(1/z)$ (eqs. (126)–(127)); map to $\theta$ (eqs. (128)–(134)). This route needs a physical gauge with a positive-norm Hilbert space and a uniform gap for $t_0\to\infty$ ("although no one can prove it!") — **not used** in our formulation. Route 2: continuation at finite staple length (App. F). Route 3 (used): Wightman functions — consecutive increments lie in the forward tube $\mathbb R^4-iV_+$, and the proper complex Lorentz transformation $\Lambda(t,z)=(iz,it)$ connects the two configurations (the source cites the "edge of the wedge" theorem; the result used is the Bargmann–Hall–Wightman theorem — terminology slip, App Q row Q.16; print "Bargmann–Hall–Wightman" (`Streater:1989vi` Thm. 2-11: spectral condition and real Lorentz covariance suffice); `Liu:2022nnk` App. F writes the map as "$\Lambda(t,z)\to(it,iz)$", which is not a Lorentz transformation — never print it; `Liu:2023pjb` eq. (3.25) states the covariance correctly).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] perturbatively (bottleneck `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt`); "analyticity is shown order by order; that the full result shares the analyticity is natural to expect" (Liu) — the non-perturbative statement is [C]. Relation to CSS: `Collins2011`, `CollinsMetz2004` assert time-ordering insensitivity and universality across $e^+e^-$, DY, SIDIS; this theorem supplies the analytic mechanism. Priority (A03 confirmed, item A3): only with 「据我们所知」 — `Liu:2022nnk` gave a systematic analytic-continuation classification of off-light-cone TMD soft functions; analyticity of the cusp anomalous dimension in the cusp angle goes back to `KorchemskyRadyushkin1987`.
* **Figure** `fig:yzliu-theta-strip` (N11). **Table** `tab:yzliu-theta`.

#### §23.4 `sec:yzliu-rapidity`

* **命题 `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear` — 离光锥软函数中快度发散的因子化（线性性）** [A].
  - *Statement.* For $Y+Y'\to\infty$: $\ln S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)+\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$; equivalently $S=\Psi^\dagger(b_\perp,\mu,Y')\Psi(b_\perp,\mu,Y)$ with $\Psi$ the rapidity-renormalized light-cone wave function of the static $Q\bar Q$ pair (`Liu:2022nnk` eq. (116)) and $|\Psi(b_\perp,\mu,Y)|=e^{\tilde KY+\mathcal D_1-\frac12\mathcal D_2}$, hence $\mathcal D=2\mathcal D_1-\mathcal D_2$ ($\mathcal D_2$ = the rapidity-independent part of the on-light-cone soft function; the source's lecture notes write $\mathcal D_0$ for it — use $\mathcal D_2$ as in `Ji:2019sxk`). This is the all-order argument for `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity`.
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, prop:tmd-evolution, lem:yzliu-partial-exp, prop:appB-webs}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.4 pp; the Cauchy step in full): route 1 (regions): power-counting parameter $e^{-(Y+Y')/2}$; leading regions = two collinear subgraphs + one soft subgraph; trivial hard part (on-shell eikonal lines). Route 2 (`Liu:2022nnk` App. E, read): axial gauge $n\cdot A=0$, $n=\hat z+\vec n_\perp$ (no Glauber problem; collinear $A^\pm$ cannot enter hard parts); split webs into self-interaction webs and 2PI vertex webs; soft factorization + replica trick (web contributions are linear in the replica number) ⇒ vertex webs carry no rapidity divergence; the divergent parts obey ${\rm div}_1(Y+Y')={\rm div}_2(Y)+{\rm div}_2(Y')$, and continuity + Cauchy's functional equation give linearity (eqs. (E.1)–(E.4)); a rapidity-slicing estimate at fixed order $n$, $|V(Y)-V(Y')|\lesssim(Y-Y')e^{-Y'/n}$, gives convergence (eqs. (E.5)–(E.13)).
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: complete except one explicitly assumed step — IR control of the highest-rapidity group ("Assuming this can be done", App. E) — the weakest step (power counting, E-S). Agreement: the claim of `CollinsSoper1981` (A04 corrected: Liu's remark that a detailed explanation beyond one loop "is not presented" concerns `Collins:2011ca`, APPB §4.4; his criticism of `CollinsSoper1981` itself is a different one — a heuristic IR-safety argument with a loophole for linear divergences, APPB App. D), Vladimirov's conformal-map renormalization theorem for light-like regulators (`Vladimirov:2017ksc`; Ch24 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm`, pointer), explicit three-loop soft functions (`Li:2016axz`).
* **定义 `def:yzliu-intrinsic-soft-lc` — 本征软函数的光锥定义** (no tag).
  - $\sqrt{S_I(b_\perp,\mu)}=\lim_{\delta^+\to0}\frac{\sqrt{S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\delta^-=\delta^+)}}{S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\hat z)}$, with $S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\hat z)$ built from one $\delta$-regulated light-like staple and one space-like $\hat z$ staple subtracted by $\sqrt{Z_E}$ (`Liu:2022nnk` eqs. (72), (76)); the $\delta$ regulator as in `rem:tmd-schemes`. `% VF-YZL-2`
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:tmd-soft, rem:tmd-schemes}`.
* **定理 `thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft` — 本征软函数：定义的等价、方案无关性与反常维度** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) $S_I$ of `def:yzliu-intrinsic-soft-lc` equals $S_r$ of `def:lamet-reduced-soft`. (b) Writing $S_1(b,\delta,Y')=e^{(Y'-\ln\delta)\tilde K+\mathcal D_1}$ and $S_2(b,\delta,\delta')=e^{-\ln(\delta\delta')\tilde K+\mathcal D_2}$: $\mathcal D=2\mathcal D_1-\mathcal D_2$; $S_I$ is rapidity-scheme independent although $\mathcal D$, $\mathcal D_1$, $\mathcal D_2$ separately are not (check: $\frac12\ln S_I=\frac12\ln S_2-\ln S_1$ gives $\mathcal D_1=\frac12(\mathcal D+\mathcal D_2)$). (c) $S_I$ is the (squared) light-cone wave function of a static $Q\bar Q$ pair at separation $b_\perp$. (d) To all orders $\mu^2\frac{d\ln S_I(b_\perp,\mu)}{d\mu^2}=\gamma_{HH}(\alpha_s)$ (§F0.3 row 10); one loop $S_I=1-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi L_b$. `% VF-YZL-2, VF-YZL-3`
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-intrinsic-soft-lc, def:lamet-reduced-soft, prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear, thm:yzliu-analyticity, def:sudakov-cusp, prop:appB-cusp-oneloop}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.3 pp): (a), (b) from `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear`, the half-on/half-off continuation `thm:yzliu-analyticity`(ii) and the region analysis (`Ji:2019sxk` eqs. (19)–(21); `Ji:2019ewn` eqs. (18)–(21); `Liu:2022nnk` eqs. (51), (76)–(81), (120)–(122); `Ji:2021znw` eqs. (46)–(48), (69)–(72); read); (d) multiplicative renormalization of cusped Wilson loops and the large-angle limit $\Gamma_{\rm cusp}(\alpha_s,\theta)=\theta\Gamma_{\rm cusp}+\gamma_{HH}+O(e^{-\theta})$ (VF-YZL-1).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (consequences of the rapidity factorization and of the region analysis); two-loop values: `prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop`. Attribution (A03 confirmed, item A5): $S_I$ was introduced by JLL as an operator-defined, rapidity-scheme-independent and Euclidean-computable object; an undetermined non-perturbative soft mismatch factor $g^S_q$ had been introduced earlier by `EbertStewartZhao2019`.
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop` — $S_I$ 的两圈结构** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$；两圈。）For $S_I$ of `def:yzliu-intrinsic-soft-lc` — a ratio of multiplicatively renormalized cusped Wilson loops — the two-loop anomalous dimension is the large-angle constant of the two-loop cusp anomalous dimension (an explicit fixed-order fact; the all-order RGE is `thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft`(d), [A], a pointer here). With $\mu^2\frac{d\alpha_s}{d\mu^2}=-\frac{\beta_0}{4\pi}\alpha_s^2$, $\beta_0=\frac{11}3C_A-\frac43T_Fn_f$:
    $$\gamma_{HH}=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi+\frac{\alpha_s^2}{\pi^2}C_F\Big[C_A\Big(-\frac{49}{36}+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{\zeta_3}2\Big)+\frac59T_Fn_f\Big]+O(\alpha_s^3),$$
    $$\ln S_I=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi L_b+\alpha_s^2\Big[-\frac{C_F\beta_0}{8\pi^2}L_b^2+\hat\gamma_{HH}L_b+c_2\Big]+O(\alpha_s^3),\qquad\hat\gamma_{HH}=\frac{C_F}{\pi^2}\Big[C_A\Big(-\frac{49}{36}+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{\zeta_3}2\Big)+\frac59T_Fn_f\Big],$$
    $c_2$ not fixed by the RGE. `% VF-YZL-3`
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-intrinsic-soft-lc, def:sudakov-cusp, prop:appB-cusp-oneloop}`.
  - *Proof.* Full for the logarithmic structure (`ex:yzliu-sI-rge`); $\gamma^{(2)}_{HH}$ from the large-angle limit of the two-loop velocity-dependent cusp anomalous dimension (`Becher:2009kw`, `KorchemskyRadyushkin1987`) with the zero-recoil check $\Gamma(0)=0$ (YZLIU `check_GammaS2.py`, $10^{-18}$); the $n_f$ term also from the running-coupling argument; agrees with `Ji:2023pba` (A.6) and `JiEtAl2021`. The three-loop constant is not printed (unverified).
  - *Verification (v2.3).* App Q rows Q.12, Q.13, Q.15 (E08, E09, E11 confirmed twice): E08 (sign of the two-loop $\Gamma_S$ in `Liu:2022nnk` eq. (81) and in `Ji:2021znw`, journal eq. (73) = eq. (72) of arXiv:2106.05310v1 — the journal version corrected the CS exponent but not $\Gamma_S^{(2)}$; the correct value is in `Ji:2023pba` (A.6)), E09 (RGE signs in `JiEtAl2021` eqs. (205)–(206), arXiv:2004.03543v2 numbering), E11 ($L_b^2$ coefficient a factor 2 too large in `Liu:2022nnk` (81) and `JiEtAl2021` (208); the single-logarithm error is in `Liu:2022nnk` (81) only). Print the correct forms with `%` comments.
* **例 `ex:yzliu-sI-rge` — 由 RGE 确定两圈 $L_b^2$ 系数** (no tag). Apply $\mu^2\frac d{d\mu^2}$ to the ansatz $\ln S_I=-\frac{\alpha_s(\mu)C_F}\pi L_b+\alpha_s^2[cL_b^2+\hat\gamma L_b+c_2]$: the term $-\frac{C_F}\pi\mu^2\frac{d\alpha_s}{d\mu^2}L_b=\frac{C_F\beta_0}{4\pi^2}\alpha_s^2L_b$ must cancel $2c\alpha_s^2L_b$, so $c=-\frac{C_F\beta_0}{8\pi^2}$. `\uses{prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop}`. 0.15 pp.
* **假设 `asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` — 光锥极限对 ${\rm Im}\,\theta$ 的一致性** [P]. Register entry: Part VII annex §F1.2 (= `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §3).
  - (UL) The light-cone limit $Y\to\infty$ of the single-time-ordered soft functions is uniform in ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in[-\pi,\pi]$, so that the light-cone form $e^{(Y+Y')\tilde K+\mathcal D}$ continues across the strip.
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-hyperbolic-angle, thm:yzliu-analyticity}`.
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-universality-classes` — 软函数的普适类** [P].
  - *Statement.* In the light-cone limit only three independent objects remain: the light-cone wave function $\Psi$ of a static $Q\bar Q$ pair, the TMDPDF $f^{\rm TMD}$ of a single heavy-quark state, and the heavy-quark TMD fragmentation function $D^{\rm TMD}$. `tab:yzliu-classes` (Liu's Table 2, transcribed from the rendered page): form factor (single $T$; arbitrary/arbitrary; ultrasoft yes; $\Psi^\dagger\Psi$; Euclidean yes) · space-like DY (double; space-like past/past; yes; $\Psi^\dagger\Psi$; yes) · time-like DY (double; time-like past/past; no; $f^{{\rm TMD}\dagger}f^{\rm TMD}$; yes) · $\gamma^*\to\bar QQ$+hadrons (double; time-like future/future; no; $D^{{\rm TMD}\dagger}D^{\rm TMD}$; no) · time-like SIDIS (double; time-like past/future; no; $D^{\rm TMD}f^{\rm TMD}$; no) · space-like SIDIS (double; space-like past/future; yes; $\Psi^\dagger\Psi$; yes) · quasi-TMD (double; time-like past/space-like arbitrary; yes; $\Psi^\dagger f^{\rm TMD}$; yes) · quasi-fragmentation (double; space-like arbitrary/time-like future; yes; $D^{\rm TMD}\Psi$; no). Universality between the time-like double-ordered types (heavy-quark TMDPDF vs fragmentation) is **not** claimed.
  - `\uses{thm:yzliu-analyticity, prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear, asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: analyticity + light-cone limits (`Liu:2022nnk` §4.5–4.7, Table 2).
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: grade fixed by (UL), verified perturbatively only for ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in\{0,\pm\pi/2,\pm\pi\}$; the author is "not clear … whether the heavy-quark TMDPDF and fragmentation functions are equal to all orders" (§6). It turns the folklore "the regulator choice does not matter" into a sharp classification and shows where universality can fail (time-like double-ordered, fragmentation, $e^+e^-$). Priority (A03, item A3): 「据我们所知」 wording only; cusp-angle analyticity goes back to `KorchemskyRadyushkin1987`.
* *Budget of §23.4.* 1.6 pp.

#### §23.5 `sec:yzliu-time-ordering`

* **定义 `def:yzliu-exp-soft` — 指数正规子下的软因子** (no tag).
  - With light-like directions (Liu's $n$, $\bar n$; the writer fixes the correspondence with our $n_A$, $n_B$, §F0.3 row 16) and the exponential regulator $\nu>0$ (`Li:2016axz`):
    $S(b_\perp,\nu,\varepsilon)=\frac1{N_c}{\rm Tr}\langle\Omega|\bar T[W_n^\dagger(-i\nu e_t,\vec b_\perp)W_{\bar n}(-i\nu e_t,\vec b_\perp)]\,T[W^\dagger_{\bar n}(0)W_n(0)]|\Omega\rangle$ (DY shape, `Liu:2023pjb` eq. (3.7)),
    $S_t(b_\perp,\nu,\varepsilon)=\frac1{N_c}{\rm Tr}\langle\Omega|T\,W_n^\dagger(-\nu e_z+\vec b_\perp)W^\dagger_{\bar n+}(-\nu e_z+\vec b_\perp)W_{\bar n+}(0)W_n(0)|\Omega\rangle$ (single $T$, eq. (3.20));
    full versions $S^{\rm full}(L^+,L^-,b_\perp,\nu,Y,\varepsilon)$ with finite light-front lengths, off-light-cone tilt $Y$ ($n_Y=n-e^{-Y}\bar n$, $v=n+e^{-Y}\bar n$), $Z$-factor subtractions and transverse links along $\vec b_\perp-i\nu e_t$ or $\vec b_\perp-\nu e_z$.
  - `\uses{def:tmd-soft, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **引理 `lem:yzliu-matrix-tree` — 坐标空间参数函数的正性（全子式矩阵树公式）** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* In the coordinate-space Schwinger parametrization of gluon $m$-point functions, the coefficient functions $P_{ij}(\alpha)$ of the quadratic form are non-negative, by an all-minors matrix-tree formula (`Liu:2023pjb` eq. (2.10), App. A; `Bogner:2010kv`). The writer copies the precise statement from App. A.
  - `% uses: none`.
  - *Proof.* Full (cite the all-minors matrix-tree theorem; 0.2 pp).
* **引理 `lem:yzliu-final-state` — 末态求和恒等式** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* For distinct real $E_k>0$ and $\nu>0$: $\int\frac{dE}{2\pi i}e^{-\nu|E|/\sqrt2}\Big[\prod_k\frac1{E-E_k-i\eta}-\prod_k\frac1{E-E_k+i\eta}\Big]=\sum_ke^{-\nu E_k/\sqrt2}\prod_{j\ne k}\frac1{E_k-E_j}$ ($\eta\to0^+$). `% VF-YZL-12`
  - `% uses: none`.
  - *Proof.* Full (partial fractions $\prod_k\frac1{E-e_k}=\sum_k\prod_{j\ne k}\frac1{e_k-e_j}\frac1{E-e_k}$ with $e_k=E_k\pm i\eta$, then $\frac1{x-i\eta}-\frac1{x+i\eta}\to2\pi i\delta(x)$; checked for $n=2,3,4$ by YZLIU `check_HL_and_C1.py`).
* **定理 `thm:yzliu-time-ordering` — DY 形 TMD 软因子的时序无关性** [A].
  - *Statement.* In DR ($\varepsilon\ne0$), linear covariant gauges, order by order in PT: (1) $S(b_\perp,\nu,\varepsilon)$ admits a below-threshold (cut-free, Euclidean-type) parametric representation built from gluon Wightman functions whose invariant length squares are $x^2_{A,ij}=-2\lambda^R_i\lambda^R_j$, $x^2_{B,ij}=-\nu^2-b_\perp^2-\sqrt2i\nu(\lambda^L_i-\lambda^R_j)$, $x^2_{C,ij}=-\nu^2-b_\perp^2-2\lambda^L_i\lambda^R_j-\sqrt2i\nu(\lambda^L_i-\lambda^R_j)$ — every imaginary part comes with a real part $\le-b_\perp^2-\nu^2<0$; (2) $S(b_\perp,\nu,\varepsilon)=S_t(b_\perp,\nu,\varepsilon)$: the DY soft factor, an amplitude squared, equals a space-like form factor of a light-like $Q\bar Q$ pair; (3) master equality $S^{\rm full}(L^+,L^-,b_\perp,\nu,Y,\varepsilon)=S_t^{\rm full}(-iL^-,-iL^+,b_\perp,\nu,Y,\varepsilon)$ (`Liu:2023pjb` eq. (3.71); unequal lengths eq. (3.54)), from which the identities for the off-light-cone, finite-LF-length and exponential regulators follow by taking limits in different orders; (4) by multiplicative renormalization the bare identities imply the renormalized ones. Consequences stated by the author: universality of the TMD soft factor, hence of the CS kernel, between inclusive TMDPDFs and exclusive LFWFs; the exponential regulator can be used for LFWFs; the Euclidean route to $S_I$ via form factors is put on a firmer basis; the $e^+e^-$ soft factor (operator order $T\bar T$, "much less local") is not covered. `% VF-YZL-12`
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-exp-soft, asm:yzliu-wightman-pt, lem:yzliu-matrix-tree, prop:appB-webs}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.7 pp) with a boxed 「Wightman 预备知识」 paragraph (forward tube; permuted extended tube $S^P_n$; complex Lorentz covariance (BHW, `Streater:1989vi`); Schwinger functions; below-threshold region $E'_n$, eq. (2.11); path-connectedness of $S^P_n\cap E'_n$, App. B): (1) enumerate all invariant length squares between points on the $T$ and $\bar T$ parts; (2) for $S_t$, Wick-rotate $\lambda\to-i\lambda$ and $\alpha\to-i\rho$ without exponential growth (eqs. (3.21)–(3.24)), equivalently apply $\Lambda(t,z)=(iz,it)$ with $\Lambda(-i\lambda n)=\lambda n$, $\Lambda(-i\lambda\bar n)=-\lambda\bar n$, $\Lambda(-\nu e_z)=-i\nu e_t$, $\Lambda v=in_Y$, $\Lambda v'=-i\bar n_Y$ (eqs. (3.25)–(3.32), (3.58)); (3) $Z$ factors and transverse links make the finite-length identities gauge invariant and remove boundary terms at light-front infinity; (4) positivity of $P_{ij}(\alpha)$ (`lem:yzliu-matrix-tree`). Source: `Liu:2023pjb` (read in full, v6 = published).
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: the most careful all-order argument available for this property; the combinatorial part is rigorous; the remaining gap is `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` ("the author believes in it", §4 item 2). Interpretation caveat stated by the author: "it is outside the scope of this paper to show that this definition correctly captures soft contributions to the DY process" (fn. 5). Non-perturbative extension: `conj:yzliu-complex-wilson`. Context (A03 item A6: 「据我们所知，`Liu:2023pjb` 首次给出了明确的全阶论证（以维数正规化坐标空间解析性为条件），说明指数正规子中的 DY 软因子与时序无关」; A04 precisions applied): time-ordering insensitivity in the finite-LF-length and $\delta$ regulators was used earlier (`Vladimirov:2016dll`, `Vladimirov:2017ksc`); some authors state it directly (`Collins2011`, `CollinsMetz2004`, `Echevarria:2015byo`), others are cautious (`Moult:2018jzp`); `Vladimirov:2017ksc` calls the rapidity factorization of the SIDIS soft factor "the open question" (JHEP version, p. 32; the sentence is not in arXiv v2; as quoted in `Liu:2023pjb`). Over-claim to avoid: 「时序无关性定理是严格的」.
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-dy-sidis-exp` — 指数正规子下 DY 与 SIDIS 软因子相等** [P].
  - *Statement.* In the exponential regulator with infinitely long light-like lines the DY and SIDIS soft factors are equal (`Liu:2023pjb` App. C): first heuristically in light-front perturbation theory, with real final-state sums by `lem:yzliu-final-state`; second by deforming to time-like and space-like lines at finite lengths. In general $S_t\ne S_{\rm SIDIS}$.
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-exp-soft, lem:yzliu-final-state, thm:yzliu-time-ordering}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: the argument is heuristic ($\pm i\eta$ tracking), not by analyticity; compare `thm:yzliu-analyticity`(iv) for the $\delta$ regulator and `thm:tmd-sivers-sign` for the operator-level T-reversal.
* **猜想 `conj:yzliu-complex-wilson` — 复时空中的 Wilson 圈** [C].
  - *Statement.* In full QCD, for closed Wilson loops $C$ whose points lie in the permuted extended tube, $\langle{\rm Tr}\,W(C)\rangle$ exists and is invariant under proper complex Lorentz transformations (`Liu:2023pjb` §3.4).
  - `\uses{asm:yzliu-wightman-pt, thm:yzliu-time-ordering}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]; it would extend `thm:yzliu-time-ordering` beyond perturbation theory.
* **Figure** `fig:yzliu-complex-lorentz` (N12). *Budget of §23.5.* 1.6 pp.

#### §23.6 `sec:yzliu-quasi-tmd`

* **命题 `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount` — 准 TMD 因子化的 Collins 式证明：单线记号下的幂次计数** [A].
  - *Statement.* Order by order in PT with on-shell massless external partons, for the quasi-TMD of `def:lamet-quasi-tmd`, under the local hypotheses (QT1)–(QT7) below: (i) leading regions follow from the Landau equations — one external collinear direction; collinear and staple displacements decouple; the staple propagators admit non-zero Feynman-parameter solutions only via dipole $2\to2$ insertions (the pinch poles of `prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft`); (ii) in single-line notation (a staple pair = one line of mass dimension $\frac32$ and spin $0$) the power of $\lambda_{\rm pc}\equiv1/(b_\perp P^z)$ is $\lambda_{\rm pc}^{F(H)+F(SC)+F(H')+F(H'')}$ with
    $$F(H)=\sum_{s=0,1}\big[N(CH,1,s)+N(SH,1,s)\big]+N(CH,\tfrac32,\tfrac12)+\tfrac32\sum_{s=0,\frac12}N(SH,\tfrac32,s)-N(CH,1,\ell)-\tfrac72,\qquad F(SC)=\tfrac12N(SC,\tfrac32,\tfrac12)+N(SC,1,0),\quad F(H')=F(H'')=0,$$
    ($N(XY,d,s)$ = number of lines of mass dimension $d$ and spin $s$ between subgraphs $X,Y$; $\ell$ = longitudinally polarized collinear gluons): $F(H),F(SC)\ge0$ (no super-leading regions); the leading region has $N(CH,\frac32,\frac12)=2$, $N(SH,\frac32,0)=1$ (one soft "staple"), any number of longitudinal collinear gluons into $H$, any number of soft gluons into $C$, nothing else ($F(H)=-\frac72+2+\frac32=0$; each extra soft gluon into $H$ costs $+1$); (iii) after the soft-to-collinear (Grammer–Yennie-type) approximation and Ward identities, in any on-light-cone regulator, $\tilde f=H\,\frac{f(x,b_\perp,\mu,\delta^-)}{S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\delta^-)}\,S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\hat z)$ (`Liu:2022nnk` eq. (74)); (iv) combined with `thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft` this reproduces `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` (with the exponent $\frac12\tilde K$; momentum evolution $P^z\frac{d}{dP^z}\ln\tilde f=\tilde K+\mathcal G$, $\mathcal G^{(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(1-L_\zeta)$). `% VF-YZL-8, VF-YZL-5`
  - *Local hypotheses* (the source's (Q1)–(Q7) renamed): (QT1) Landau-equation leading regions (App. C); (QT2) collinear directions other than $p$, possible in individual cut diagrams, cancel by the sum over cuts; (QT3) no Glauber region: inside the hard region all small-$k^z$ link poles can be deformed into the upper half-plane, and the same deformation avoids $k^+k^-\ll k_\perp^2$; (QT4) pinch poles and ultrasoft modes as in `prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft`; (QT5) hard subgraphs homogeneous, all components $\sim P^z$; (QT6) multiplicative renormalizability of the staple operator (`asm:lamet-staple`(ST1)); (QT7) perturbation theory with on-shell massless partons.
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, asm:lamet-staple, prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft, thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft, prop:tmd-regions}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.5 pp): `Liu:2022nnk` eqs. (54)–(68), App. C (read in full); one-loop checks: APPB (39), (23), (85). Figure `fig:yzliu-leading-region` (N10).
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: power-counting steps E-S; the PLB version was explicitly a "sketch of mathematical proof"; promotion to [R] needs a rigorous multi-scale analysis of singular Minkowski integrals ("simultaneous decomposition of the longitudinal and transverse phase space", APPB §6). Attribution (A03 item B1): 「据我们所知」, Ji–Liu–Liu (`Ji:2019ewn`) first matched a quasi-TMD to the scheme-independent TMDPDF with a Euclidean-computable soft factor; the single-line power counting is `Liu:2022nnk` eqs. (54)–(68).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-hard-kernel` — 准 TMD 匹配核：RGE、重—轻分解与两圈结果** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) $\mu\frac d{d\mu}\ln H\big(\frac{\zeta_z}{\mu^2}\big)=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}(\alpha_s)\ln\frac{\zeta_z}{\mu^2}+\gamma_C(\alpha_s)$, $\gamma_C\equiv\tilde\gamma_H=\gamma_V+2\gamma_F+\gamma_{HH}=\gamma_V+2\gamma_F+2\gamma_{HL}-\gamma_s$ ($\gamma_V$: non-cusp AD of the light-quark Sudakov form factor; $\gamma_F$: heavy–light current; $\gamma_{HL}$: constant of the heavy–light cusp; $\gamma_s$: light-like soft constant; `Ji:2023pba` (A.11)); the PLB form $2\gamma_F+\Gamma_S+2\gamma_H$ and the APPB form $2\gamma_F-\Gamma_S+2\gamma_J$ agree once §F0.3 row 10 is applied. (b)（单圈，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）$H=1+a\big(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{L_\zeta^2}2+L_\zeta\big)=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{4\pi}\big(-L_\zeta^2+2L_\zeta-4+\frac{\pi^2}6\big)$, $H=C^+_{HL}C^-_{HL}$, $C^\pm_{HL}=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{8\pi}\big(-4-\frac{5\pi^2}6+2L_\pm-L_\pm^2\big)$, $L_\pm=L_\zeta\pm i\pi$; the pair $(C^\pm_{HL})^2$ is the LFWF kernel $H^\pm_{\rm LFWF}$ with one-loop phase $\frac{\alpha_sC_F}2(1-L_\zeta)$ up to the ${\rm sgn}(z)$ convention; $\gamma^{(1)}_C=-\frac{C_F}\pi\alpha_s$. (c)（两圈，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）in the JHEP 2023 convention ($d/d\ln\mu$, $\beta_0^{(b)}=\frac{11C_A}{6\pi}-\frac{n_f}{3\pi}$): $\tilde\gamma^{(2)}_H=\frac{1188\zeta_3-99\pi^2-1108}{432\pi^2}C_FC_A-\frac{36\zeta_3+6-7\pi^2}{12\pi^2}C_F^2+\big(\frac1{24}+\frac{10}{27\pi^2}\big)C_Fn_f$; $\ln H^{(2)}=\frac{C_F\beta_0}{24\pi}L_\zeta^3-\frac14\big(\Gamma^{(2)}_{\rm cusp}+\frac{C_F\beta_0}{2\pi}\big)L_\zeta^2-\big(\frac{C_F\beta_0}{4\pi}(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12})+\frac{\tilde\gamma^{(2)}_H}2\big)L_\zeta+c_H$, $c_H=0.0725\,C_F^2-0.0840\,C_FC_A+0.1453\,C_Fn_fT_F$ (closed form VF-YZL-7). `% VF-YZL-6, VF-YZL-7`
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount, def:sudakov-cusp, prop:lattice-renormalizability}`.
  - *Proof plan.* (a) RG consistency of `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` (sketch); (b) full (`ex:yzliu-hl-decomposition`); (c) cite `Ji:2023pba` (A.13), (5.7), (5.11) (read) and the direct NNLO computation `delRio:2023pse` (read) — in `Ji:2023pba` $c_H$ was *extracted* from the NNLO quasi-PDF comparison (`prop:yzliu-threshold-nnlo`), and the direct NNLO quasi-TMD coefficient agrees. Checks (report): (A.11) = (A.13) given (A.2)–(A.5) (`check_gammaC2.py`), $c_H$ numerics, log structure from the RGE (`check_RGE_structure.py`), $n_f$ parts = independent large-$\beta_0$ results of `Liu:2023onm` (2.41)–(2.42).
  - *Status and gap.* RGE structure [A]; (b), (c) [R, R_PT] at the stated orders. Erratum: `Ji:2019ewn` eq. (26) gives a wrong $\gamma^{(2)}_C$; the correct value is printed in `Ji:2023pba` (A.12)–(A.13), which however cites the earlier value without noting the discrepancy (claim E10 confirmed; App Q, row Q.14).
* **例 `ex:yzliu-hl-decomposition` — 单圈 $H=C^+_{HL}C^-_{HL}$ 与相位** (no tag). $L_++L_-=2L_\zeta$, $L_+^2+L_-^2=2L_\zeta^2-2\pi^2$ ⇒ $(C^+C^-)^{(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{4\pi}(-4+\frac{\pi^2}6+2L_\zeta-L_\zeta^2)=H^{(1)}$; ${\rm Im}\,C^{+(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}4(1-L_\zeta)$, so $(C^+)^2$ has phase $\frac{\alpha_sC_F}2(1-L_\zeta)$. `\uses{prop:yzliu-hard-kernel}`. 0.15 pp.
* *Budget of §23.6.* 1.3 pp.

#### §23.7 `sec:yzliu-exclusive`

* **命题 `prop:yzliu-lfwf` — 准光前波函数振幅的因子化** [A].
  - *Statement.* (i) Rapidity-renormalized LFWF amplitudes $\psi^\pm_N(x_i,\vec b_{i\perp},\mu,\zeta)$, defined with generalized soft functions $S^\pm_N$, obey $2\zeta\partial_\zeta\ln\psi^\pm_N=\tilde K_N$ and an RGE; the future/past versions are related by analytic continuation, e.g. $S^{-\dagger}_N(\delta^+\delta^-)=S^+_N(-\delta^+\delta^-+i0)$. (ii) Quasi-LFWF amplitudes factorize as $\tilde\psi^\pm_N\sqrt{S_{rN}}=\exp\big[\frac12\ln\frac{\mp\zeta_z-i0}\zeta\,\tilde K_N\big]H^\pm_N\psi^\pm_N+\dots$; for the $q\bar q$ meson amplitude the one-loop rapidity logarithms differ by $\frac12\tilde K\ln(\zeta_z/\zeta)$ since $\sqrt{\zeta_z\bar\zeta_z/(\zeta\bar\zeta)}=\zeta_z/\zeta$; $H^\pm_N$ has an imaginary part that does not cancel (unlike the TMDPDF, where the phases of the two Sudakov factors cancel); for the meson, $H^\pm_{\rm LFWF}=(C^\pm_{HL})^2$. (iii) $\tilde K_N$ follows from ratios of quasi-LFWFs at two momenta. Conceptual remark (untagged, in text): light-front quantization as an effective theory with small-$k^+$ modes integrated out; LaMET as "effective light-front quantization" (`Ji:2021znw` §II.C).
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, prop:yzliu-hard-kernel, thm:yzliu-time-ordering}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (`Ji:2021znw` §§II–V, read; "a sketch of its proof is included"); the $\pm i0$ choices are fixed by exponential decay at light-front infinity.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]; universality of $\tilde K$ between LFWFs and TMDPDFs is supported by `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`. *Verification outcome:* the factor $\frac12$ is present in the journal version of `Ji:2021znw` (eqs. (49), (51), (74); its footnote 1 notes the omission in `JiEtAl2021` and in one more reference); only arXiv:2106.05310v1 lacks it — cite the journal version; `Ji:2021znw` is therefore not an App Q item (claim E02 confirmed twice; the other sources are App Q row Q.11).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-meson-ff` — 大动量下的介子形状因子与 $S_I$ 的提取** [A].
  - *Statement.* For $F(b_\perp,P,P',\mu)=\langle P'|\bar\eta(\vec b_\perp)\Gamma'\eta(\vec b_\perp)\,\bar\psi(0)\Gamma\psi(0)|P\rangle$ with $P,P'$ approaching opposite light-cone directions and $\Gamma=\Gamma'=1$ or $\gamma_\perp$, at large $P^z$ and $b_\perp\gg1/P^z$:
    $$F(b_\perp,P,P',\mu)=S_I(b_\perp,\mu)\int dx\,dx'\,H(x,x')\,\tilde\psi^\dagger(x',b_\perp)\tilde\psi(x,b_\perp)+\dots,\quad H=\frac{H_1(Q^2,\bar Q^2,\mu^2)}{H_2(\zeta_z,\bar\zeta_z)H_2(\zeta'_z,\bar\zeta'_z)},\quad H_1=H^{\rm Sud}(-Q^2)H^{\rm Sud}(-\bar Q^2),$$
    $Q^2=\sqrt{\zeta_z\zeta'_z}$, $\bar Q^2=\sqrt{\bar\zeta_z\bar\zeta'_z}$, $\tilde\psi$ the quasi-TMD wave functions of `prop:yzliu-lfwf`; hence $S_I=F/\int H\tilde\psi\tilde\psi^\dagger$ is lattice accessible. `% VF-YZL-16`
  - *Local hypothesis.* TMD factorization of this exclusive form factor in the Li–Sterman spirit (two hard cores at $0$ and $\vec b_\perp$, two collinear subgraphs, one soft subgraph; Glauber exchanges handled as in DY/SIDIS, `lem:tmd-glauber`; all gauge links future-pointing).
  - `\uses{prop:yzliu-lfwf, thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft, lem:tmd-glauber}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: `Liu:2022nnk` §5, eqs. (143)–(155) (read; leading-region analysis, Fig. 25), `Ji:2019sxk` eqs. (14)–(21) (read; there the factorizations of $F$ and $\tilde\psi$ are stated with details "in a future publication"); the relation $2\mathcal D_1-\mathcal D_2=\mathcal D$ and the master formula are verified at one loop.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] for the factorization (weakest step: the Glauber treatment of an exclusive form factor, argued by analogy with DY/SIDIS; the frontier LaMET report, based on the 2020 paper only, grades the relation [P] — this outline follows the later full argument of `Liu:2022nnk` §5 but records the disagreement); one-loop checks are evidence. Interpretation: a gauge-invariant completion of the Collins–Soper axial-gauge factorization; with $x+x'=1$ imposed the end-point behaviour improves to $x^2\ln^2x$. Lattice: `LatticeParton:2020uhz` (leading-order factorization; "the first lattice calculation of $S_I$" only as LPC's own claim, with 「据我们所知」 (A03 confirmed, item I.5); the first lattice CS-kernel determination is `Shanahan:2020zxr`), `LatticePartonLPC:2023pdv` (one-loop corrections, abstract), `Deng:2022gzi` (one-loop TMDWFs and soft functions).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-dy-quasi` — 以准 TMD 表示的小 $q_T$ Drell–Yan 截面** [A].
  - *Statement.* $\frac{d\sigma_{\rm DY}}{d^2\vec b_\perp\,dx\,dx'}=\hat\sigma(x,x',\mu,\zeta_z,\zeta'_z)\,S_I(b_\perp,\mu)\,\tilde f(x,b_\perp,\mu,\zeta_z)\tilde f(x',b_\perp,\mu,\zeta'_z)+\dots$, with $\mathcal H(Q^2/\mu^2)=H(\zeta_z/\mu^2)H(\zeta'_z/\mu^2)\hat\sigma$ ($\mathcal H$ the hard factor of `thm:tmd-factorization`); the CS factors cancel when $\zeta_z\zeta'_z=\zeta_A\zeta_B=Q^4$; no light-cone limit is needed.
  - `\uses{thm:tmd-factorization, thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Full given the two theorems (algebra; 4 lines). Sources `Ji:2019sxk` eq. (22), `Ji:2019ewn` eq. (27), `Liu:2022nnk` eq. (155) (read). Fourier sign: Liu writes $d\sigma\propto\int d^2b\,e^{i\vec b\cdot\vec Q}$ — check $b$-odd structures against §F0.3/row 30.
  - *Status and gap.* [A], conditional on `thm:tmd-factorization` ([A], incl. its Glauber argument) and `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` ([A]).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-sivers` — 由准 TMD 之比得到 Sivers 不对称** [A].
  - *Statement.* The matching kernel is spin independent (only hard gluons), so the ratio of the quasi-Sivers to the unpolarized quasi-TMD at the same $(x,b_\perp,P^z)$ equals the light-cone ratio up to power corrections ($S_I$ and $H$ cancel); the DY/SIDIS sign follows from the staple direction $\pm\hat z$, consistent with `thm:tmd-sivers-sign`. Evidence: at one loop in the small-$b_\perp$ limit the quasi-Sivers function reproduces the collinear twist-3 (Qiu–Sterman) description; hard-gluon-pole contributions (colour factor $C_A/2$) partly cancel the soft-gluon-pole ones ($1/(2N_c)$), leaving $C_F$ for the end-point and pinch-pole terms.
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, prop:lamet-tmd-ratios, thm:tmd-sivers-sign}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (sketch; `Ji:2020jeb` §§I–II, structure of §III read); the one-loop check is evidence. Fourier sign conventions for $b$-odd structures (§F0.3 / WRITER_SPEC_v2 row 30).
* *Budget of §23.7.* 1.1 pp. (Near-threshold quarkonium photoproduction of Guo–Ji–Liu belongs to Ch19 — pointer only, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2.)

#### §23.8 `sec:yzliu-threshold`

* **定义 `def:yzliu-hl-jet` — 重—轻 Sudakov 因子与类空喷注函数** (no tag).
  - (a) $C^\pm_{HL}$: the single-vertex heavy–light Sudakov factor (a light on-shell quark turned into a static space-like link); JLS's "$H_{HL}$" denotes the pair; $H=|H_{HL}|=C^+_{HL}C^-_{HL}$ (`prop:yzliu-hard-kernel`). (b) Space-like jet function $\tilde J(\mu z)=\langle\Omega|\mathcal T\,W_{n,+}(zn_z)W^\dagger_z(zn_z)W_z(0)W_{n,-}(0)|\Omega\rangle$ (light-like links $W_{n,\pm}$, space-like link $W_z$; a "transition form factor" of a light-like into a space-like static source) — real, below threshold, dependent on $|z|$ only, conjugate to the semi-hard scale $|1-\xi|p^z$; time-like analogue $\tilde J(\mu t)$ ($n_z\to n_t$). (c) $J_f(z\mu)\equiv\tilde J(z^2\mu^2)e^{i\,{\rm sgn}(z)A(L_\zeta)}$ with ${\rm sgn}(z)A={\rm Arg}\,H_{HL}$. Conventions: our operator $\bar\psi(z)\gamma^0W[z,0]\psi(0)$ coincides with JLS's, so ${\rm sgn}(z)$ means the same; JLS's $\lambda=zp^z=-\lambda_{\rm ours}$; their $L_z$, $l_z$ are our $L_\zeta$, $L_z$. `% VF-LAM-14, VF-YZL-9`
  - `\uses{def:prelim-wilson, def:lattice-quasi, prop:yzliu-hard-kernel}`.
* **定理 `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation` — 类空与类时重夸克喷注函数的解析延拓** [A].
  - *Statement.* For $t>0$ and to all orders in PT (DR): $\tilde J(t=-i|z|,D)=\tilde J(z,D)$. Hence the two-loop space-like jet function follows from the time-like heavy-quark jet function (`Jain:2008gb`). One loop: $\tilde J=1+a\big(\frac12L_z^2+L_z+\frac{\pi^2}{12}+2\big)$, $\frac{d\ln\tilde J}{d\ln\mu}=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_z-\tilde\gamma_J$, $\tilde\gamma^{(1)}_J=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi$; two loops $\tilde\gamma^{(2)}_J=C_FC_A(-\frac{1396}{27}+\frac{23\pi^2}9+20\zeta_3)+C_Fn_f(\frac{232}{27}-\frac{2\pi^2}9)$ in units of $(\alpha_s/4\pi)^2$. `% VF-YZL-9`
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-hl-jet, asm:yzliu-wightman-pt}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: proof 1 (`Ji:2023pba` App. C.1, read): coordinate-space Schwinger representation of all $m$-point gluon correlators; contractions preserve $\int D\alpha\,\mathcal P(\alpha)\exp[-\frac i2\sum Q_{ij}(x_i-x_j)^2]$ with $Q\ge0$; all residue poles of the remaining scale parameter $s$ lie in quadrant II, so $s\to-is$ is allowed, then $t\to-iz$; spin structures match term by term (eqs. (C.19)–(C.21)); proof 2 (App. C.2): Wightman functions in the forward tube + complex Lorentz covariance (`asm:yzliu-wightman-pt`). One-loop RGE: check by hand; $\tilde\gamma^{(2)}_J$ from (A.4)–(A.5).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (the same type of argument as `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, with a full combinatorial sketch for proof 1); $\tilde\gamma_J$ equals the time-like heavy-quark jet-function anomalous dimension.
* **定理 `thm:yzliu-threshold` — 准 PDF 匹配核的阈值因子化（Ji–Liu–Su）** [A].
  - *Statement.* For the non-singlet $\overline{\rm MS}$ kernel ($\Gamma=\gamma^0$) of `thm:lattice-lamet`, at leading power in $1-\xi$ (all terms $\propto(1-\xi)^{-1}\ln^k|1-\xi|$ and $\delta(1-\xi)$), to all orders:
    $$C\Big(\xi,\frac{p^z}\mu\Big)\Big|_{\xi\to1}=H\Big(\frac{4p_z^2}{\mu^2}\Big)\,p^z\,J_f\Big(\frac{(1-\xi)p^z}\mu,\frac{4p_z^2}{\mu^2}\Big)\big[1+O(1-\xi)\big],\qquad p^z=|y|P^z,$$
    with $H=|H_{HL}|$ — **the same kernel as in the quasi-TMD and quasi-LFWF factorizations** (universality) — and $J_f$ of `def:yzliu-hl-jet`. One loop: $H^{(1)}=a(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{L_\zeta^2}2+L_\zeta)$, $\tilde J^{(1)}=a(\frac{L_z^2}2+L_z+\frac{\pi^2}{12}+2)$, ${\rm Im}\,J^{(1)}_f=a\pi\,{\rm sgn}(z)(L_\zeta-1)$; momentum space $p^zJ_f=\delta(1-y)\big[1+a\big(2+\frac{\pi^2}4+\frac{L_\zeta^2}2-L_\zeta\big)\big]+a\big[\mathcal P\frac{\ln(1-y)^2}{|1-y|}+(L_\zeta-1)\big(\mathcal P\frac1{1-y}+\mathcal P\frac1{|1-y|}\big)\big]$. RGEs: $\frac{d\ln H}{d\ln\mu}=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_\zeta+\tilde\gamma_H$, $\frac{d\ln\tilde J}{d\ln\mu}=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_z-\tilde\gamma_J$, $\frac{d\ln H_{HL}}{d\ln\mu}=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_\zeta+\tilde\gamma_H-i\pi\,{\rm sgn}(z)\Gamma_{\rm cusp}$; at one loop the summed anomalous dimension $\Gamma_{\rm cusp}[\ln(\lambda^2e^{2\gamma_E})-i\pi\,{\rm sgn}\,z]$ is $\mu$ independent; DGLAP consistency at threshold: `prop:yzliu-ad-algebra`. `% VF-LAM-14`
  - `\uses{def:yzliu-hl-jet, thm:yzliu-jet-continuation, prop:yzliu-hard-kernel, prop:lamet-oneloop, thm:lattice-lamet}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.5 pp): (1) leading region (JLS fig. 1): collinear quark lines with hard vertices at the two quark–link junctions (scale $\zeta_z=4p_z^2$) and semi-hard gluons at $|1-\xi|p^z$ exchanged between the collinear quark and the static link, the analogue of DIS at $x\to1$ (`Sterman1987`, `BecherNeubertPecjak2007`; Ch13 `prop:thr-dis` for the QCD analogue); space-time picture: two hard real exchanges with opposite $k_z=O(p_z)$ are phase-space suppressed; (2) $\tilde J$ is a vacuum Wilson-loop correlator related to the time-like heavy-quark jet function (`thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`); (3) RG consistency reproduces the threshold limit of DGLAP; (4) fixed order: one loop exact (`ex:yzliu-threshold-oneloop`; LAMET s3 to $|\lambda|=10^5$ incl. the sign of the phase for $\lambda<0$; claim N06, a verification), NNLO `prop:yzliu-threshold-nnlo`. Source `Ji:2023pba` eqs. (1.3), (2.1)–(2.7), (3.17)–(3.19), (4.5)–(4.10), (6.1)–(6.19), Apps. A, C (read in full).
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: a leading-region argument with a space-time picture plus RG consistency (SCET type), not a full Collins-style power-counting proof; the phase is the new element relative to DIS threshold factorization. Attribution (A03 item C2): 「据我们所知」, `Ji:2023pba` first derived the threshold factorization $C\to H\cdot J$ of the momentum-space kernel and its RG resummation; earlier `Gao:2021hxl` explained the origin of the threshold logarithms and resummed those of the pseudo-PDF coefficient at NLL.
* **例 `ex:yzliu-threshold-oneloop` — 阈值因子化的单圈检验** (no tag). For $0<y<1$ the $\overline{\rm MS}$ quasi-PDF behaves as $\frac{2a}{1-y}(L_\zeta+\ln(1-y)-1)$ (`prop:lamet-oneloop`(e)) $=J^{(1)}_f$, using $\mathcal P\frac1{|1-y|}+\mathcal P\frac1{1-y}=\frac2{1-y}$; for $y>1$ it gives $\frac{2a\ln(y-1)}{y-1}=J^{(1)}_f$ because the two principal values cancel; $H^{(1)}$ coincides with the quasi-TMD kernel of `prop:yzliu-hard-kernel`(b). `\uses{thm:yzliu-threshold, prop:lamet-oneloop}`. 0.2 pp.
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-ad-algebra` — 反常维度代数** [A].
  - *Statement.* In the basis $\{\Gamma_{\rm cusp},\gamma_F,\gamma_V,\gamma_s,\gamma_{HL}\}$: $\gamma_\phi=-\frac12\gamma_V-\frac12\gamma_s$, $\gamma^{\rm thr}_J=\frac12\gamma_V-\frac12\gamma_s$, $\tilde\gamma_J=2\gamma_{HL}-2\gamma_s$, $\gamma_{HH}=2\gamma_{HL}-\gamma_s$, $\tilde\gamma_H=\gamma_V+2\gamma_F+2\gamma_{HL}-\gamma_s$; DGLAP consistency at threshold $2\gamma_\phi+\tilde\gamma_H-\tilde\gamma_J=2\gamma_F$ (end-point form $P_{qq}\to\frac{2\Gamma_{\rm cusp}}{(1-z)_+}+2\gamma_\phi\delta(1-z)$). (两圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$) the two-loop inputs (A.1)–(A.5) of `Ji:2023pba` reproduce $\gamma^{(2)}_{HH}$ (independently `prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop`) and $\tilde\gamma^{(2)}_H$ (`prop:yzliu-hard-kernel`(c)); $\gamma_V$ is the standard quark form-factor non-cusp anomalous dimension. `% VF-YZL-10`
  - `\uses{thm:yzliu-threshold, prop:yzliu-hard-kernel, thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft, thm:yzliu-jet-continuation}`.
  - *Proof.* Full for the identities given the factorization formulas (substitution: $2\gamma_\phi+\tilde\gamma_H-\tilde\gamma_J=2\gamma_F$ identically); inputs cited.
  - *Status.* Structural relations [A] (they follow from the consistency of [A] factorization formulas); two-loop values [R, R_PT].
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-threshold-nnlo` — 阈值因子化的 NNLO 检验** [P].
  - *Statement.* The threshold limit of the exact two-loop non-singlet kernel (`Li:2020xml`, `Chen:2020ody`) reproduces all 41 non-vanishing singular terms ($\delta(1-y)$ and $\ln^k(1-y)/(1-y)$, $k\le3$) of `thm:yzliu-threshold`, with two constants extracted from the comparison: $c_a$ (two-loop phase) and $c_H$ (two-loop $\ln H$); the independently computed NNLO quasi-TMD coefficient (`delRio:2023pse`) agrees with this $c_H$.
  - `\uses{thm:yzliu-threshold, prop:yzliu-hard-kernel, prop:lamet-twoloop}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: a fixed-order verification with two fitted constants (`Ji:2023pba` §5, eqs. (5.7), (5.10)–(5.11), read).
* **注 `rem:yzliu-threshold-domain` — 阈值重求和与大 $x$ 适用域** (no tag). Resummed kernel (TR, `Ji:2023pba` eq. (6.19) with the star distribution (6.18); $\mu_i=|1-\xi||xP^z|$, $\eta=2a_\Gamma(\mu_i,\mu)$; their $l_z$ = our $L_z$): $C=H(\alpha(\zeta_z))\exp[2S(\zeta_z,\mu)-2S(\mu_i,\mu)-a_H(\zeta_z,\mu)+a_J(\mu_i,\mu)]\tilde J(L_z=-2\partial_\eta,\alpha(\mu_i))\big[\frac{\sin({\rm sgn}(1-\xi)\hat A+\eta\pi/2)}{|1-\xi|}\big(\frac{2|1-\xi||xP^z|}{\mu_i}\big)^\eta\big]_\star\frac{\Gamma(1-\eta)e^{-\eta\gamma_E}}\pi$, $\hat A=A(\alpha(\zeta_z))+\pi a_\Gamma(\zeta_z,\mu)$; the perturbative jet function requires $\Lambda\ll|1-\xi||xP^z|\ll|xP^z|$. Numerical lesson (`Ji:2024hit`, abstract): with a hard part at $xP^z$ and a threshold soft function at $(1-x)P^z$, resummation of $\alpha_s^k\ln^{2k}(1-x)$ is "crucial"; matching breaks down when $(1-x)P^z\to\Lambda$ and works when both $xP^z$, $(1-x)P^z\gg\Lambda$; LRR combines with threshold resummation. Earlier Mellin-space NLL: `Gao:2021hxl` (abstract). GPDs at threshold: `Holligan:2025baj` (abstract). Refines `rem:lamet-domain`.
* **Figure** `fig:yzliu-threshold` (N13). *Budget of §23.8.* 1.6 pp.

#### §23.9 `sec:yzliu-renormalon`

* **命题 `prop:yzliu-linear-renormalon` — 重—轻 Sudakov 核的线性红外重整子** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在大 $\beta_0$（泡链）近似中，」 the Borel transform of the $\overline{\rm MS}$ heavy–light kernel (`Liu:2023onm` eqs. (2.44)–(2.47); Borel variable $u=\beta_0^{(b)}t/2$ = our $u$, §F0.3 row 11) has its first right-half-plane singularity at $u=\frac12$, **in the imaginary part only**: ${\rm Im}\,B(u)\to\frac\mu{p^z}\frac{C_Fe^{5/6}}{2\pi}\frac{{\rm sgn}(z)}{u-\frac12}$; the phase angle has the asymptotics $A_{\rm asym}=-2\frac\mu{p^z}\sum_nr_n\alpha_s^{n+1}$, $r_n=N_m(\beta_0^{(b)})^n\Gamma(n+1)$, $N_m=\frac{C_Fe^{5/6}}\pi\simeq0.977$ (the normalization of the pole-mass and Wilson-line linear renormalon); the real part has none; the light–light Sudakov kernel has none. It cancels (i) in the threshold expansion of space-like quark-bilinear coefficient functions (coordinate and moment space) against the UV renormalon of an explicit next-to-leading-power threshold soft operator, and (ii) in the TMD expansion of the quasi-LFWF against the UV renormalon of an NLP soft factor $S^\pm_q$ containing the dimension-1 Wilson-line cusp operator $\hat O=[n_z\infty,0]\int_{-\infty}^0dx^+[0,x^+]D_\perp^2[x^+,-\infty]$ (eqs. (4.28)–(4.33)); the renormalon is tied to the linear UV divergence, not to the linear rapidity divergence; at bubble-chain level the TMD expansion of the quasi-LFWF is not convergent (alternating residues, exponentially small terms); the quark field renormalization in Coulomb gauge also has a linear renormalon. `% VF-YZL-11`
  - `\uses{prop:yzliu-hard-kernel, def:power-borel, prop:power-renormalon-examples, prop:lamet-lrr}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (`Liu:2023onm` §§1–2, 3.6, 4.3–4.4, 6, read); residue and $N_m$ checked by hand. Heuristic origin: analytic continuation of the linear static potential, $gA^z\sim iV$, giving $-i\frac{\delta m}{2p^z}$ corrections.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (exact within the bubble chain). Consequence: the slow convergence of the imaginary parts of lattice CS-kernel extractions (`prop:lattice-cs-kernel`); Borel-resummed hard kernels reduce it. Background `Beneke1999`, `Braun:2018brg`.
* **猜想 `conj:yzliu-renormalon-norm` — 线性重整子归一化的普适性** [C]. The same $N_m$ holds beyond large $\beta_0$ ($N_m=0.622,\dots,0.524$ for $n_f=0,\dots,5$; `Liu:2023onm` eq. (2.52)). `\uses{prop:yzliu-linear-renormalon}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: proposed in `Liu:2023onm` (eq. (2.52)) by analogy with the pole-mass/Wilson-line linear renormalon; only the bubble-chain value is derived (`prop:yzliu-linear-renormalon`); no argument beyond large $\beta_0$ for the heavy–light kernel. Upgrade: a beyond-bubble-chain analysis of the heavy–light kernel.
* **猜想 `conj:yzliu-threshold-hadronic` — 强子夸克双线性矩阵元的阈值渐近** [C]. A fully factorized threshold ($\lambda\to\pm i\infty$; the writer converts Liu's sign) asymptotics of the hadronic quark-bilinear matrix element at moderate $z$, with a non-perturbative threshold soft factor $J(z\Lambda_{\rm QCD})$ (`Liu:2023onm` eq. (3.50)). `\uses{prop:yzliu-linear-renormalon, thm:yzliu-threshold}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: an ansatz of `Liu:2023onm` (eq. (3.50)); it presupposes a hadron-level version of `thm:yzliu-threshold` ([A] only for partonic kernels) and a non-perturbative threshold soft factor $J(z\Lambda_{\rm QCD})$; no argument at hadron level. Upgrade: a hadron-level threshold factorization (cf. `asm:intro-bridge`).
* **Figure** `fig:yzliu-borel` (optional). *Budget of §23.9.* 0.6 pp.

#### §23.10 `sec:yzliu-gpd`

* **命题 `prop:yzliu-gpd-oneloop` — 准 GPD 单圈匹配核在 $x=\pm\xi$ 附近的结构** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$；单圈。）In the one-loop quasi-GPD matching kernel the terms sensitive to the would-be soft scales $2|x\pm\xi|P^z$ are $\big(\frac1{2(y-x)}\mp\frac1{4\xi}\big)\frac{|x\pm\xi|}{y\pm\xi}\ln\frac{4P_z^2(x\pm\xi)^2}{\mu^2}$ plus virtual $\delta(x/y-1)$ terms (TR, `Su:2026eta`† eq. (7) from the rendered page; the writer checks which of $x$, $y$ is the quasi variable and re-derives from the one-loop kernel or hedges 「（据原文，待核实）」); the logarithm of the would-be soft scale is multiplied by $|x\pm\xi|$ and vanishes as $x\to\mp\xi$ — the analogue of DVCS at $x_B=\xi$, where the double logarithms in $\xi-x_B$ come multiplied by $(x_B-\xi)$ and the soft region associated with the real photon is power suppressed (`JiOsborne1998`, `CollinsFreund1999`, `Radyushkin1997`, as cited there).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-gpd, def:excl-gpd}`.
  - *Proof plan.* **Full at one loop, mandatory**: re-derive the structure from the explicit one-loop quasi-GPD kernel (the source `Su:2026eta` is a preprint 「（预印本）」 and its formula is transcribed, TR). If the writer cannot re-derive it, the tag falls back to [A] by the [R]† rule of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4 and the statement is reported (integrator decision). The all-order matching `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd` ([A]) is a pointer, not a premise.
* **猜想 `conj:yzliu-gpd-xi` — GPD 在 $x=\pm\xi$ 附近的 LaMET 可计算性** [C].
  - *Statement.* Near $x=\pm\xi$ quasi-GPD matching receives no leading-twist contribution from the soft scales associated with $(x\pm\xi)P^z$; the requirement $2|x\pm\xi|P^z\gg\Lambda$ can be replaced by $2(|x|+|\xi|)P^z\gg\Lambda$ and $2|1\pm x|P^z\gg\Lambda$; GPDs extracted with complete one-loop matching are continuous at $x=\pm\xi$ with discontinuous derivatives (with the regulator ordering $\epsilon_2\ll|\epsilon_1|\to0$; other orderings diverge; earlier apparent discontinuities "may arise from regulator-related numerical artifacts"; reanalysis of four lattice data sets) (`Su:2026eta`†, read in full).
  - `\uses{prop:yzliu-gpd-oneloop, thm:lamet-quasi-gpd}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C] — open (attribution A07 confirmed; `Su:2026eta` find no leading-twist soft contribution and GPDs continuous at $x=\pm\xi$ with discontinuous derivatives, in four lattice data sets with the regulator ordering $\epsilon_2\ll|\epsilon_1|\to0$; neither paper criticises the other): renormalon analysis predicts non-perturbative corrections at $x=\pm\xi$ suppressed by only the first power of the hard scale, from soft momenta, unrelated to the Wilson-line UV renormalon (`Braun:2024snf`, abstract); kinematic twist-3 contributions are logarithmically enhanced and discontinuous at $x=\pm\xi$ (`Braun:2023alc`, abstract). The positions are not strictly contradictory (no leading-twist soft term vs a power correction enhanced to $1/P^z$). A proof needs an all-order power counting of the soft-quark (zero-mode) region of the quasi-GPD at $x=\pm\xi$, analogous to `CollinsFreund1999`.
* *Budget of §23.10.* 0.5 pp.

#### §23.11 `sec:yzliu-models`

* **定理 `thm:yzliu-gn-convergence` — 二维 $U(N)$ Gross–Neveu 模型中扭度展开的绝对收敛** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在二维 $U(N)$ Gross–Neveu 模型中，于 $1/N$ 展开的次领头阶，」 the valence "structure function" $\langle p,i|\bar\psi^i(x)\psi^i(0)|p,i\rangle-\langle p,i|\bar\psi^j(x)\psi^j(0)|p,i\rangle=\bar u(p)u(p)\,\mathcal E(z^2m^2,\lambda)$ (Liu's variables: $z^2=-x^2>0$, $\lambda=-p\cdot x$; convert per §F0.3 row 17) admits an explicit twist expansion in the Bjorken limit $z^2\to0$ at fixed $\lambda$ to all powers, and this expansion converges absolutely and pointwise for every $0<z^2<\infty$ and $\lambda\in i\mathbb R_{\ge0}$ (imaginary Ioffe time, in his convention). `% VF-YZL-13`
  - `% uses: none`.
  - *Proof plan.* Model-internal proof (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.10 item 5): sketch of `Liu:2024omb` §4 (read): double Mellin representations in $s$ (coupling) and $u$ (w.r.t. $z^2m^2/4$), eqs. (2.4), (2.6)–(2.15); Gamma-function and ${}_1F_1$ inequalities (4.2)–(4.5); remainder bound (4.7); dominated sums (4.11), (4.13) — complete for $F_1$, "similar" for $F_2$, $F_3$. Checks (report): YZLIU `check_GN_ineq.py` (4000 random points; all ratios $\le1$); (4.4) also follows from the product formula for $|\Gamma(x+iy)|$.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] as a QCD statement; within the model rigorous at $O(1/N)$ modulo the parts stated to be "similar". Significance (quote): "the specific structure of the QCD Bjorken limit commonly called 'collinear factorization', strictly speaking, has no exact derivation up to now" (`Liu:2024omb` §1); to our knowledge (A03 item D1) this is the first exactly worked marginal (asymptotically free) 2D theory — U($N$) Gross–Neveu at $O(1/N)$ — in which the Bjorken-limit twist expansion of a forward matrix element is derived to all powers and shown to converge pointwise in coordinate space; the all-power convergent OPE of a vacuum correlator was obtained earlier in the O($N$) σ model (`Beneke:1998eq`). Only **imaginary** Ioffe time is covered (cf. `asm:lamet-uniform`). Over-claim to avoid: 「二维 Gross–Neveu 模型的结果证明了 QCD 的扭度展开收敛」.
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-gn-structure` — Gross–Neveu 模型中扭度展开的结构：重整子相消与真空凝聚** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在同一模型（$1/N$ 次领头阶）中，」 (i) hard functions $H^{l,p}_k$ and PDF-like non-perturbative functions $q^{l,p}_k$, $\hat q^{(l)}_k$ appear as Borel integrals to all powers; (ii) renormalon poles at $t=n$ cancel between the hard function at power $p$ and the non-perturbative function at power $p+n$, for each $l$ (at $t=0$: the logarithmic UV–IR cancellation, the source of scale/scheme dependence); (iii) the leading-power non-perturbative functions are given explicitly in coordinate space and in momentum-fraction space $0<\alpha<1$, with "zero-mode-type" subtractions at $\alpha=0$; (iv) threshold expansion to next-to-next-to-leading threshold power; resurgence between threshold and "Regge" asymptotics; (v) at NLP (and through $g_0^2\langle\bar\psi\psi\rangle$ already at LP) sea-quark operators sensitive to vacuum condensates enter: "the internal structure of the fermion and the vacuum can not be cleanly separated … in the strict sense the collinear factorization in the partonic picture is invalid, but the OPE expansion always works".
  - `\uses{thm:yzliu-gn-convergence, def:power-borel}`.
  - *Status.* [P] (model; `Liu:2024omb` §§1–2.1, 4, 7, read).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-on-factorial` — 超可重整二维 $O(N)$ 模型：动量空间 OPE 的阶乘增强** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在超可重整的二维 $O(N)$ 四次模型中，于 $1/N$ 的次领头阶，」 the large-$p^2$ OPE of the scalar two-point function has factorial enhancements of high-power coefficient functions (introduced by IR subtractions in fixed-point PT) and of condensates, e.g.
    $$\mathcal H_{0,0,s_1=n}(p^2)\sim\frac1{(p^2)^n}\Big(\frac g{4\pi}\Big)^{n+1}\frac{d^n}{dt^n}\Big[\Big(\frac{m^2}{p^2}\Big)^t\psi(1-n-t)-\frac1t\Big]_{t=0}\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}-n!\frac{g^{n+1}}{(4\pi)^{n+1}}\frac{m^2}{(p^2)^{n+1}}+n!\frac{(-1)^ng^{n+1}}{(4\pi)^{n+1}}\frac1{m^2(p^2)^{n-1}}+\dots;$$
    the enhancements cancel between coefficient functions and condensates only off-diagonally across powers, so the momentum-space power expansion diverges (a single Borel prescription does not cure it); in coordinate space a $1/(n!)^2$ suppression keeps it convergent; $\overline{\rm MS}$ renormalization factors introduce no factorial growth. `% VF-YZL-14`
  - `\uses{def:power-borel}`.
  - *Status.* [P] (model; `Liu:2025edu`, abstract and §§1, 4 read). Relevance: the remainder of `thm:lamet-hadronic` is a momentum-space power expansion — asymptotic at best (`rem:lamet-open` item 13).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-nlsm` — 二维非线性 $\sigma$ 模型：凝聚与跨级数** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在二维 $O(N)$ 非线性 $\sigma$ 模型中，于 $1/N$ 的次领头阶，」 a manifestly $O(N)$-symmetric massless perturbative framework (a limit of the quartic linear $\sigma$ model) reproduces the perturbative two-point function and its first exponentially small correction, from the condensate of the Lagrangian (trace-anomaly) operator, in full agreement with the exact large-$N$ solution; the first positive-axis Borel singularity ($y=2$) of the perturbative self-energy is a **UV** renormalon cancelled by the ambiguity of the condensate. The fourth-order free energy agrees with the TBA (`Liu:2026npv`†, abstract).
  - `\uses{def:power-borel}`.
  - *Status.* [P] (model; `Liu:2025bqq`, abstract and §§1, 5). Complements `prop:power-renormalon-examples` (O($N$) $\sigma$ model, GN Borel summability).
* **命题 `prop:yzliu-2dqcd` — 二维大 $N_c$ QCD 中的准 PDF** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在二维标量与旋量 QCD 的 $1/N_c$ 展开中，」 the quasi-PDF of a meson tends to the light-cone PDF as $P\to\infty$ at leading and subleading order in $1/N_c$, without additional renormalization (2D QCD is super-renormalizable).
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, prop:power-thooft-model}`.
  - *Status.* [P] (model; `Ji:2018waw`, §§I–II and conclusion read). Related (abstracts): 't Hooft-model quasi-PDFs/DAs — light mesons approach the light cone more slowly than heavy quarkonia (`Jia:2018qee`); quasi-GPDs (`Jia:2024atq`; read by verify s1: it also corrects the quasi-PDF expression of `Jia:2018qee` for flavour-neutral mesons at $O(N_c^0)$, a self-correction whose extra term vanishes as $P\to\infty$); light-front sum rules regular at $x=0$ (`Ji:2020bby`).
* **注 `rem:yzliu-transforms` — 坐标空间与动量空间之间变换的教训** (no tag). Fourier transforming coordinate → momentum is safer than the reverse; correct at fixed order and under Borel representations; "questionable" for RG-resummed expressions not convertible to Borel form; truncated Borel integrands can produce fake renormalons; in planar 2D QCD the small-$z^2$ expansion has infinite radius while the large-$p^2$ expansion grows factorially but is Borel summable (`Liu:2024omb`, read).
* **注 `rem:yzliu-instantons` — 瞬子与模型研究** (no tag). Small-instanton contributions to quasi-PDF matching kernels as part of a trans-series (`Liu:2021evw`†; model dependent; no numbers printed); pion/kaon quasi-PDFs in the instanton vacuum with factorization assumed (`Kock:2020frx`, abstract).
* *Budget of §23.11.* 1.4 pp.

#### §23.12 `sec:yzliu-decay`

* **命题 `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions` — 标量场论中的谱表示与指数衰减（微扰论各阶）** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在 $\varphi^3$ 与 $\varphi^4$ 标量理论中，微扰论各阶，」 the forward correlator at arbitrary external momentum has a spectral representation with spectral support $x\in(-1,1)$ ("a result of locality"), and it decays exponentially at large space-like separation (from parametric representations, `Ji:2026vir`† §5.3); two-loop $\varphi^3$ checks of the asymptotic forms.
  - `% uses: none`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (scalar theories, perturbative; 「（预印本）」; `Ji:2026vir` §§1–2, 5, 6 read). Text (untagged): the same paper extends `thm:lamet-exp-decay` to channels with one-particle intermediate states — through spectral representations of form factors and their "locality completion" the decay rates become $M$, $\sqrt{s_0}m$ or $m_c(s_0,m,M)$ (eqs. (5.21)–(5.23)), and locality cancels the final-state form-factor singularities; this extension has the status of `thm:lamet-exp-decay` (an implication under axioms whose content grade is [C] through the spectral hypotheses) and is not graded separately. Relevance: the Fourier step of Ch22 (`lem:lamet-strip`, `prop:lamet-truncation`) and the forward/inverse problem debate.
* *Budget of §23.12.* 0.4 pp.

#### §23.13 `sec:yzliu-status`

* **注 `rem:yzliu-relation-css` — 与 CSS、Collins 与 SCET 的关系** (no tag). Taken over without re-proof: TMD factorization of DY/SIDIS with Collins' soft factor (`thm:tmd-factorization`, [A]), CS evolution, the cancellation of Glauber exchanges and final-state interactions in inclusive processes. Beyond CSS: Euclidean realizations of soft physics; new factorization theorems for lattice-computable objects; lemmas that CSS-type arguments use implicitly (time ordering, analyticity, universality classes, rapidity factorization); threshold and renormalon structure of the heavy–light kernel; exact model laboratories; axiomatic tools (coordinate-space analyticity, tubes, complex Lorentz covariance, clustering). SCET derivations (`Vladimirov:2020ofp`, `Ebert:2022fmh`, `delRio:2023pse`) agree with `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` once the lattice soft factor is identified with $S_I$. Global context: `Scimemi:2019cmh` (global TMD extractions whose large-$b_\perp$ input the lattice programme aims to supply), `Collins:2023cuo` ($e^+e^-$ soft factor in a Wightman framework — open).
* **Table `tab:yzliu-status`.** label | statement | tag (+ qualifier) | bottleneck | error type | refereed?
* **注 `rem:yzliu-open` — 开放问题** (no tag). (1) [R]-level quasi-TMD/TMD factorization (multi-scale Minkowski analysis); (2) `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` as a theorem; (3) `conj:yzliu-complex-wilson` and the uniform-gap/light-cone hypotheses (`asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform`); (4) universality of double-time-ordered time-like soft functions; the $e^+e^-$ soft factor; (5) polarized and gluon quasi-TMDs in the $S_I$ framework (partly `Ebert:2022fmh`); (6) linear-renormalon normalization beyond large $\beta_0$; the hadronic threshold conjecture; (7) convergence vs asymptoticity of 4D twist expansions (`thm:yzliu-gn-convergence` vs `prop:yzliu-on-factorial`); (8) all-order control of ultrasoft/"ultra-ultrasoft" modes and of the IR step of `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear`; (9) GPDs at $x=\pm\xi$ beyond one loop.

### Misconception box (Ch23)
C9 (TMD universality is derived from operator definitions, not assumed); C10 (only combined objects are regulator independent; the "bent" Euclidean soft function fails from two loops); M15 (the soft factor is not 1 for TMDs); M16 (rapidity divergences are physical: the $Y$-linear term of a physical heavy-quark form factor is the CS kernel); 「TMD 软函数本质上是闵氏的，因此不能在格点上计算」 (`thm:yzliu-soft-hqet`, `prop:yzliu-meson-ff`); 「软因子的时序总是无关紧要」 (no: time-like double-ordered and fragmentation-type soft functions, `prop:yzliu-universality-classes`); 「二维模型证明了 QCD 扭度展开收敛」 (no: `thm:yzliu-gn-convergence` is a model result, and momentum-space expansions can diverge, `prop:yzliu-on-factorial`).

# PART 1b — Part VII global decisions and annexes F1/F4/F5/F6 (verbatim)

## Part VII — global decisions (the former frontier §F0; binding for Ch22–Ch24)

### F0.1 Scope, budgets, format

| # | key | file | 中文标题（binding） | pp | format |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 22 | `lamet` | `ch22_lamet.tex` | LaMET 与格点因子化：季向东等人的因子化证明 | 18 | frontier |
| 23 | `yzliu` | `ch23_yzliu.tex` | Yizhuang Liu 的严格因子化研究 | 15 | frontier |
| 24 | `front` | `ch24_front.tex` | 其他前沿严格结果 | 18 | frontier |

Frontier format (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §11, §6.11): ≈55 % statements with hypotheses and proof architecture, ≈20 % explicit results (one-/two-loop kernels, checks, worked examples), ≈25 % status, evidence and open problems. Every chapter opens with the box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (§6.6) and ends with a status table and an open-problem list. No correspondence table is required (§6.7 lists only core chapters).

### F0.2 Ownership decisions (binding for this outline)

1. **Ch22 `lamet`** owns: the LaMET principle; lattice moments and the Euclidean Compton amplitude (v1 `prop:lattice-moments`); quasi-/pseudo-distributions and lattice cross sections (v1 `def:lattice-quasi`); support and analyticity; renormalization of straight space-like Wilson-line operators (v1 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`); renormalization schemes incl. the hybrid scheme; one- and two-loop kernels; the matching theorems with the hypotheses (H1)–(H5) of the LaMET report (now `prop:lattice-renormalizability` and `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-uniform`, `asm:lamet-twist4`, `asm:lamet-large-z`) and the three proof routes (OPE route of Izubuchi–Ji–Jin–Stewart–Zhao; Ma–Qiu collinear-subtraction route; good lattice cross sections); v1 `thm:lattice-lamet`; target-mass and dynamical power corrections; the linear renormalon of the Wilson-line self-energy and leading-renormalon resummation; the Fourier step incl. the exponential-decay theorem (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2, VF-LAM-18); LaMET vs short-distance factorization (SDF); quasi-GPD/DA; **the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, the off-light-cone and reduced soft functions (definitions), the lattice Collins–Soper kernel (v1 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`) and TMD ratios**; v1 `rem:lattice-critiques`, `ex:lattice-quasi-tree`; the status board.
   - **Former deviation from the WRITER_SPEC_v2 v2.1 default, adopted as binding at the merge** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 v2.2 §6.11 item 5 and §12.2 now say the same; integrator decision MD9). Reason: `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` is a moved v1 `lattice` label, and the binding lead-author decision says that Ch22 absorbs the v1 `lattice` content with unchanged labels; its proof uses the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, and the no-forward-edge rule (§4.5 item 4) forbids `\uses{thm:<Ch23 label>}` in Ch22. The frontier task statement also lists "LaMET for DAs/GPDs/TMDs (Ji–Liu–Liu; Ebert–Stewart–Zhao; Vladimirov–Schäfer), lattice Collins–Soper kernel" under Ch22. Consequences: VF-LAM-15 and VF-LAM-16 are "Used in: Ch22" (`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`); `fig:yzliu-leading-region` stays in Ch23 because it illustrates Liu's Collins-style proof route (`prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`).
2. **Ch23 `yzliu`** owns: Euclidean soft functions as heavy-quark (HQET) form factors; the partial-exponentiation lemma; pinch poles and ultrasoft modes; analyticity in the complex hyperbolic angle and the universality classes; the linearity (factorization) of rapidity divergences; the intrinsic soft function $S_I$ (on-light-cone definition, scheme independence, anomalous dimension, two-loop structure); the 2024 time-ordering theorem; Liu's Collins-style power-counting proof route of the quasi-TMD theorem; the hard kernel (RGE, heavy–light decomposition, two loops); LFWF amplitudes; the meson form factor and the extraction of $S_I$; DY in terms of quasi-TMDs; Sivers ratios; the space-like jet function and its continuation; **the threshold factorization of the quasi-PDF kernel** (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2 and VF-LAM-14); the anomalous-dimension algebra; the linear renormalon of the heavy–light kernel; GPDs near $x=\pm\xi$; the exactly solvable 2D models; the extensions of the exponential-decay theorem.
3. **Ch22 does not own threshold factorization.** It owns only the one-loop endpoint structure of the matching kernel (`prop:lamet-oneloop`(e)) and the domain-of-validity remark (`rem:lamet-domain`), with forward pointers in running text to `thm:yzliu-threshold` (never in `\uses`).
4. **Ch24 `front`** owns all OTHERS.md items (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 5). It never re-states a core statement. Where a core statement already covers part of a topic, Ch24 states the frontier refinement under a new `front` label and cites the core label; the core statements keep their labels and owners. Overlaps handled this way: `thm:ir-landau-momentum`, `rem:ir-landau-status`, `prop:ir-coleman-norton`, `prop:ir-regions-minkowski`, `lem:appD-gordan` (Landau/regions); `thm:power-ope-convergence` (massive $\varphi^4_4$ OPE convergence stays in Ch21); `prop:scet-glauber-operators` (RS Glauber operators stay in Ch15); `prop:break-jet-veto`, `prop:break-fss`, `prop:break-spacelike`, `prop:break-mass-singularity`, `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18); `prop:jets-sll`, `thm:jets-ngl-factorization`, `thm:jets-bms` (Ch16); `thm:smallx-bk`, `thm:smallx-bfkl` (Ch17); `conj:heavy-nrqcd-production`, `prop:heavy-quarkonium-fragmentation` (Ch20); `conj:pdf-positivity` (Ch8); `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `rem:appJ-nonlocal`, `prop:appL-renormalized` (App J, App L); `prop:power-nlp-endpoint` (Ch21); `prop:thr-nlp` (Ch13); `prop:jets-ngl-beyond` (Ch16); `prop:dy-abelian-model` (Ch12, the eikonal spectator phase); `thm:heavy-nrqcd-decay` (Ch20). Requested core-side pointers: §F5.
5. **Exponential decay.** Ch22 owns `thm:lamet-exp-decay` (the theorem of Ji–Liu–Su under axioms) and `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` (the QCD asymptotic forms); Ch23 owns `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions` (all-order spectral representation and exponential decay in $\varphi^3/\varphi^4$, [P]); the extension of `thm:lamet-exp-decay` to one-particle intermediate states is described in the text of that entry, with the status of `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, and is not graded separately.
